They\n"},{"number":1606,"text":"deliberately show domain identities, so they do not reveal that two\n"},{"number":1607,"text":"domain-distinct components rest on the same data structures and algorithms.\n"},{"number":1608,"text":"Repeated machinery is therefore invisible to a domain-only reading, and each\n"},{"number":1609,"text":"occurrence looks correctly owned and correctly named within its own entity.\n"},{"number":1610,"text":"\n"},{"number":1611,"text":"**Component role sets.** Every material realization component has an explicit\n"},{"number":1612,"text":"set of semantic and operational roles. Technology capability does not confer\n"},{"number":1613,"text":"authority. A component's declared role set is design authority; where a role\n"},{"number":1614,"text":"carries authority, composition and use preserve the applicable authority\n"},{"number":1615,"text":"relation under `STDO-UP-004`, which is supporting law here rather than the\n"},{"number":1616,"text":"owner of the role model.\n"},{"number":1617,"text":"\n"},{"number":1618,"text":"A component may lawfully hold several roles. Three cases must be distinguished:\n"},{"number":1619,"text":"behavior already inside an abstract declared role is ordinary variation; a new\n"},{"number":1620,"text":"operational responsibility and a new semantic or authoritative role are each\n"},{"number":1621,"text":"design changes. Only the latter two require re-entry. Use of a component\n"},{"number":1622,"text":"outside its declared role set must not silently establish identity, admission,\n"},{"number":1623,"text":"lifecycle, ordering, closure, or projection truth.\n"},{"number":1624,"text":"\n"},{"number":1625,"text":"**The projection.** At a material semantic boundary, the design must project\n"},{"number":1626,"text":"the domain design into its computational realization: abstract data types,\n"},{"number":1627,"text":"identity and key structures, algorithms, algebraic laws, mutation and effect\n"},{"number":1628,"text":"boundaries, selected implementations, and domain-to-computational mappings. The\n"},{"number":1629,"text":"cross-view evaluation extends to prove the domain views and this projection\n"},{"number":1630,"text":"describe one system.\n"},{"number":1631,"text":"\n"},{"number":1632,"text":"This projection applies the build-tenant binding owned by\n"},{"number":1633,"text":"[SPEC_METHOD's WHAT, HOW, And Work Carriers](SPEC_METHOD.md#what-how-and-work-carriers).\n"},{"number":1634,"text":"The tenant design binds common requirements to executable operations and their\n"},{"number":1635,"text":"actual environment under that law. A machine stack, a human procedure and a\n"},{"number":1636,"text":"mixed process each state their operations, composition, effect locations,\n"},{"number":1637,"text":"authority and material resource measures, with their units and observation\n"},{"number":1638,"text":"boundaries. An operation performed by a person\n"},{"number":1639,"text":"retains its input, outcome, refusal and recovery contract; a role title is not\n"},{"number":1640,"text":"its implementation. The [orthogonal accounts](FOUNDATIONS.md#orthogonal-descriptions) must agree\n"},{"number":1641,"text":"on the same obligations; domain semantics supply the subject those accounts\n"},{"number":1642,"text":"describe. Software-module mechanics apply\n"},{"number":1643,"text":"where software realizes the boundary, not as a demand to disguise every human\n"},{"number":1644,"text":"step as code.\n"},{"number":1645,"text":"\n"},{"number":1646,"text":"For a material producer-to-consumer path, this projection identifies fact\n"},{"number":1647,"text":"establishment, consuming operations, applicable basis and invalidators, effect\n"},{"number":1648,"text":"boundaries, retained alternatives and accumulated work. Apply the validity and\n"},{"number":1649,"text":"reuse relation owned by [workspace and context](FOUNDATIONS.md#workspace-and-context);\n"},{"number":1650,"text":"[Spec UP-021](SPEC_METHOD.md#complete-enclosing-relation-admission-stdo-up-021)\n"},{"number":1651,"text":"adds enclosing-relation admission conditions. Method and assurance operations\n"},{"number":1652,"text":"are included where material. Component-local success cannot\n"},{"number":1653,"text":"establish the complete-path claim, and different names do not establish\n"},{"number":1654,"text":"different computational responsibilities.\n"},{"number":1655,"text":"\n"},{"number":1656,"text":"**Operational assumptions.** Material algorithmic work declares the supported\n"},{"number":1657,"text":"input dimensions, workload and growth, and operational assumptions needed for\n"},{"number":1658,"text":"the selected outcome. Bind any acceptance limits and their owning authority\n"},{"number":1659,"text":"before qualification; limits and workloads are Product-owned, not universal\n"},{"number":1660,"text":"method constants. Challenge the assumptions on representative supported paths\n"},{"number":1661,"text":"and material growth. Observe work directly where elapsed time cannot locate it,\n"},{"number":1662,"text":"without repeating the operation merely to count it. Separate material startup,\n"},{"number":1663,"text":"framework processing, external computation, transport and assurance costs so\n"},{"number":1664,"text":"that one cannot conceal another. Existing design and proof surfaces carry these\n"},{"number":1665,"text":"relations; no additional accounting system or fixed algorithmic complexity is\n"},{"number":1666,"text":"required.\n"},{"number":1667,"text":"\n"},{"number":1668,"text":"**The projection is subordinate.** It reveals repeated implementation\n"},{"number":1669,"text":"structure; it cannot decide semantic identity, ownership, or authority.\n"},{"number":1670,"text":"Computational similarity produces a candidate, never an adjudication:\n"},{"number":1671,"text":"\n"},{"number":1672,"text":"```text\n"},{"number":1673,"text":"computational similarity\n"},{"number":1674,"text":"  -> commonization candidate\n"},{"number":1675,"text":"  -> semantic and operational equivalence evaluation\n"},{"number":1676,"text":"  -> reuse, parameterized common relation, or justified separation\n"},{"number":1677,"text":"```\n"},{"number":1678,"text":"\n"},{"number":1679,"text":"Two components may share a map or an append-only sequence while differing in\n"},{"number":1680,"text":"identity and key law, ownership, ordering, admissible mutation, consistency and\n"},{"number":1681,"text":"concurrency, retention, failure semantics, access policy, lifecycle, or\n"},{"number":1682,"text":"authority over truth.\n"},{"number":1683,"text":"\n"},{"number":1684,"text":"A materially non-equivalent law among those dimensions requires separation.\n"},{"number":1685,"text":"Other differences justify separation only when evaluation shows that\n"},{"number":1686,"text":"parameterization or adaptation would weaken ownership, authority, lifecycle,\n"},{"number":1687,"text":"failure, or operational constraints. The existence of a difference does not by\n"},{"number":1688,"text":"itself discharge Prime contraction or recurrence review.\n"},{"number":1689,"text":"\n"},{"number":1690,"text":"**Disposition is two-part.** Each material algorithmic obligation in `M(B)`\n"},{"number":1691,"text":"carries a *semantic disposition* — existing accepted relation, extension, or\n"},{"number":1692,"text":"new candidate relation — and a *realization disposition* — consume, implement\n"},{"number":1693,"text":"locally, optimize, adapt, or deliberately duplicate under stated non-semantic\n"},{"number":1694,"text":"constraints. Their assurance depends on each path's justification,\n"},{"number":750,"text":"not its length or label. Requirement-derived assertions assess the requirement's\n"},{"number":751,"text":"applicable conditions; refinement-derived assertions assess their more detailed\n"},{"number":752,"text":"conditions. Neither path automatically covers the other.\n"},{"number":753,"text":"\n"},{"number":754,"text":"Assertion provenance identifies the owning obligation, interpretation,\n"},{"number":755,"text":"assumptions, derivation and shared dependencies that justify the expected\n"},{"number":756,"text":"predicate. Bind\n"},{"number":787,"text":"coverage, uncertainty and the conditions for action or acceptance. More evidence\n"},{"number":788,"text":"strengthens a claim only within its applicability and dependence account;\n"},{"number":789,"text":"test counts or an assumed preference for more checks supply neither. Required\n"},{"number":790,"text":"safety, authority and acceptance conditions remain applicable to every selected\n"},{"number":791,"text":"strategy.\n"},{"number":792,"text":"\n"},{"number":793,"text":"Quantified assurance requires a declared population or uncertainty model,\n"},{"number":794,"text":"prior information, likelihoods, shared causes and calibration limits. For a\n"},{"number":795,"text":"violation event V and evidence event E under Gamma, Bayes' rule gives:\n"},{"number":796,"text":"\n"},{"number":797,"text":"```text\n"},{"number":798,"text":"P(V | E, Gamma) =\n"},{"number":799,"text":"  P(E | V, Gamma) P(V | Gamma)\n"},{"number":800,"text":"  / [P(E | V, Gamma) P(V | Gamma)\n"},{"number":801,"text":"     + P(E | not V, Gamma) P(not V | Gamma)].\n"},{"number":802,"text":"```\n"},{"number":803,"text":"\n"},{"number":804,"text":"The denominator must be nonzero and every used conditional defined. Agreement,\n"},{"number":805,"text":"disagreement or another observation may be E; its likelihood in faulty and\n"},{"number":806,"text":"non-faulty conditions matters. The equation supplies no missing prior or\n"},{"number":807,"text":"likelihood. Its numerical conclusion is bounded by the supporting model and\n"},{"number":808,"text":"evidence; agreement can fail to increase confidence when shared causes or new\n"},{"number":809,"text":"information change that model.\n"},{"number":810,"text":"\n"},{"number":811,"text":"Let M_i mean that check i misses V under the specified conditions. Replacing a\n"},{"number":821,"text":"conditional factor with P(M_i | V, Gamma) requires the relevant conditional\n"},{"number":822,"text":"independence under that model. One minus the joint miss probability is the\n"},{"number":823,"text":"chance of at least one detection under those conditions, not the probability\n"},{"number":824,"text":"that the whole product is correct.\n"},{"number":825,"text":"\n"},{"number":826,"text":"Sampled cases or injected defects calibrate only their justified relation to\n"},{"number":827,"text":"operational failures.
